//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
                std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                    .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
            }
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    pkg.insert("name", toml_edit::value(m.package.name.clone()));
    pkg.insert("version", toml_edit::value(m.package.version.clone()));
    pkg.insert("type", toml_edit::value(m.package.kind.clone()));
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    build.insert("cpp_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.cpp_std.clone()));
    build.insert("c_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.c_std.clone()));
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        deps.insert(name, toml_edit::value(version.clone()));
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some(c) if matches!(c, '>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const FULL: &str = "\
[package]
name = \"demo\"
version = \"0.1.0\"
type = \"both\"

[build]
cpp_std = \"c++23\"
c_std = \"c11\"

[dependencies]
fmt = \"10.2.1\"
zeta = \">=1.0\"
";

    /// Deterministic scratch dir under target/ (gitignored) for file-based tests.
    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")
            .join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_full_manifest() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "bin");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_load_roundtrip() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-roundtrip");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_preserves_existing_comments() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-comments");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "# my project\n\
             [package]\n\
             name = \"demo\"\n\
             # pinned version\n\
             version = \"0.1.0\"\n\
             type = \"bin\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_validation_rules() {
        for ok in ["demo", "a", "foo-bar", "_x", "9lives", "a_b-c"] {
            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_name(bad), "{bad:?} should be invalid");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_rejects_bad_name_type_version() {
        for text in [
            "[package]\nname = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"Bad-Name\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\ntype = \"exe\"\n",
        ] {
            let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(text).unwrap();
            let err = m.validate().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(err.contains("help:"), "missing help hint in: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn conan_ref_branches() {
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", "10.2.1"), "fmt/10.2.1");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ">=10"), "fmt/[>=10]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "<=2"), "z/[<=2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "=1.2.3"), "z/[=1.2.3]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "^1"), "z/[^1]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }
}

        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_validation_rules() {
        for ok in ["demo", "a", "foo-bar", "_x", "9lives", "a_b-c"] {
            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_name(bad), "{bad:?} should be invalid");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_rejects_bad_name_type_version() {
        for text in [
            "[package]\nname = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"Bad-Name\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\ntype = \"exe\"\n",
        ] {
            let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(text).unwrap();
            let err = m.validate().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(err.contains("help:"), "missing help hint in: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn conan_ref_branches() {
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", "10.2.1"), "fmt/10.2.1");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ">=10"), "fmt/[>=10]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "<=2"), "z/[<=2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "=1.2.3"), "z/[=1.2.3]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "^1"), "z/[^1]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }
}

My `apply_to_doc` does `pkg.insert("version", toml_edit::value(...))`. `Table::insert` — does it preserve comments attached to existing keys? toml_edit stores decorations (comments) on key-value pairs. When you `insert` with an existing key, the new `Value` has no decoration (decor), and the old decorated key-value pair (including the leading comment) is replaced → the comment is lost.

   toml_edit preserves decorations when modifying "in place": `pkg["version"].as_value_mut().unwrap().as_str_mut().unwrap().clone_from(...)` — modify the existing string value in place, keeping the key and its decoration. But if the key doesn't exist, insert.

           Some(v) => {
               if let Some(s) = v.as_str_mut() { s.clone_from(value); }

           }

Let me think — in toml_edit, `Value` has `decor: Decor`, and a KeyValue is… in toml_edit 0.19+, `KeyValue` = { key: Key, value: Value }, and the leading whitespace/comment goes into the value's decor.    In toml_edit, the leading comment on a key-value line is stored in the **value's** leading decor. I recall this as a quirk: `kv.value.decor.prefix` contains "\n# pinned version\n". Hmm, or is it `kv.key`? Let me check: toml_edit's `Value { repr, value, fmt, decor: Decor }` where Decor { prefix, suffix }. For a table's value, decor.prefix = the whitespace/comments before that key-value pair. I'm fairly confident the leading comment attaches to the value's decor.prefix (which is why `doc["key"].as_value_mut().as_str_mut().clone_from(x)` preserves comments — a well-known toml_edit idiom).

   So, replacing the whole Value (`*v = value(...)`) would lose the comment decor, but modifying the string in place (`as_str_mut().clone_from`) preserves it (same Value, same decor). For the common case (string→string), in-place is the right answer.

   So:
   ```rust
   fn set_str(table: &mut toml_edit::Table, key: &str, value: &str) {
       match table.get_mut(key) {
           Some(Item::Value(v)) if v.as_str().is_some() => {
               v.as_str_mut().expect("str checked").clone_from(value);
           }
           _ => {
               table.insert(key, toml_edit::value(value.to_string()));

//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() && !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    pkg.insert("name", toml_edit::value(m.package.name.clone()));
    pkg.insert("version", toml_edit::value(m.package.version.clone()));
    pkg.insert("type", toml_edit::value(m.package.kind.clone()));
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    build.insert("cpp_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.cpp_std.clone()));
    build.insert("c_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.c_std.clone()));
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        deps.insert(name, toml_edit::value(version.clone()));
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some(c) if matches!(c, '>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const FULL: &str = "\
[package]
name = \"demo\"
version = \"0.1.0\"
type = \"both\"

[build]
cpp_std = \"c++23\"
c_std = \"c11\"

[dependencies]
fmt = \"10.2.1\"
zeta = \">=1.0\"
";

    /// Deterministic scratch dir under target/ (gitignored) for file-based tests.
    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")
            .join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_full_manifest() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "bin");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_load_roundtrip() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-roundtrip");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_preserves_existing_comments() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-comments");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "# my project\n\
             [package]\n\

             # pinned version\n\

        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();

        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_validation_rules() {
        for ok in ["demo", "a", "foo-bar", "_x", "9lives", "a_b-c"] {
            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_name(bad), "{bad:?} should be invalid");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_rejects_bad_name_type_version() {
        for text in [
            "[package]\nname = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"Bad-Name\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\ntype = \"exe\"\n",

            let err = m.validate().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(err.contains("help:"), "missing help hint in: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn conan_ref_branches() {
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", "10.2.1"), "fmt/10.2.1");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ">=10"), "fmt/[>=10]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "<=2"), "z/[<=2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "=1.2.3"), "z/[=1.2.3]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "^1"), "z/[^1]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }
}

//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() && !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    pkg.insert("name", toml_edit::value(m.package.name.clone()));
    pkg.insert("version", toml_edit::value(m.package.version.clone()));
    pkg.insert("type", toml_edit::value(m.package.kind.clone()));
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    build.insert("cpp_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.cpp_std.clone()));
    build.insert("c_std", toml_edit::value(m.build.c_std.clone()));
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        deps.insert(name, toml_edit::value(version.clone()));
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some('>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const FULL: &str = "\
[package]
name = \"demo\"
version = \"0.1.0\"
type = \"both\"

[build]
cpp_std = \"c++23\"
c_std = \"c11\"

[dependencies]
fmt = \"10.2.1\"
zeta = \">=1.0\"
";

    /// Deterministic scratch dir under target/ (gitignored) for file-based tests.
    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")
            .join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_full_manifest() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "bin");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_load_roundtrip() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-roundtrip");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

    fn save_preserves_existing_comments() {

        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");

             # pinned version\n\
             version = \"0.1.0\"\n\
             type = \"bin\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {

        }
    }

    #[test]

    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed

    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    set_str(pkg, "name", &m.package.name);
    set_str(pkg, "version", &m.package.version);
    set_str(pkg, "type", &m.package.kind);
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    set_str(build, "cpp_std", &m.build.cpp_std);
    set_str(build, "c_std", &m.build.c_std);

    }

        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some('>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

    const FULL: &str = "\

c_std = \"c11\"

    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {

            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")

    }

        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");

        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");

        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();

        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "# my project\n\
             [package]\n\

             # pinned version\n\
             version = \"0.1.0\"\n\
             type = \"bin\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();

        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();

        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));

        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }

//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() && !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    set_str(pkg, "name", &m.package.name);
    set_str(pkg, "version", &m.package.version);
    set_str(pkg, "type", &m.package.kind);
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    set_str(build, "cpp_std", &m.build.cpp_std);
    set_str(build, "c_std", &m.build.c_std);
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        set_str(deps, name, version);
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Set a string key in a table. Existing string values are mutated in place so
/// the value's decoration (leading comments) survives; other cases insert
/// fresh.
fn set_str(table: &mut toml_edit::Table, key: &str, value: &str) {
    match table.get_mut(key) {
        Some(Item::Value(v)) if v.as_str().is_some() => {
            v.as_str_mut()
                .expect("checked above")
                .clone_from(value);
        }
        _ => {
            table.insert(key, toml_edit::value(value.to_string()));
        }
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some('>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

Wait — about the borrow issue in set_str: `match table.get_mut(key) { Some(Item::Value(v)) => ... v.as_str_mut() ... }` — `v` is a `&mut Value` obtained from `table.get_mut(key)`, and while holding `v` we're calling `v.as_str_mut()` — that's fine, no second borrow of table. But in the `_ =>` arm, we're calling `table.insert` — at that point the scrutinee's borrow of table... the match's scrutinee `table.get_mut(key)` produces a `Option<&mut Item>` that's valid across the whole match, so `table.insert(...)` inside the `_` arm would be a conflict!

Hmm, wait, is that really true? `table.get_mut(key)` mutably borrows table for the lifetime of the matched Option<&mut Item>. Inside the arm, that mutable reference is live. `table.insert` needs another `&mut table` → E0499.

In the `Some(Item::Value(v))` arm, v is derived from the scrutinee's borrow, and there's no further use of table — no problem (the borrow is being used, not newly acquired).

But in the `_` arm: while the scrutinee's `Option<&mut Item>` is still live (even if matched against the wildcard), we call `table.insert`. Wildcard arm — is the scrutinee's borrow considered live in the wildcard arm? NLL: the borrow ends when the reference is no longer used. In the `_` arm, the Option<&mut Item> isn't used... but is it "live"? NLL is flow-sensitive: after the match's scrutinee evaluation, in the wildcard arm the value is unused, so the borrow could be considered dead. Actually, for `match expr { _ => ... }` where expr borrows — rustc's NLL usually allows this. Because the borrow ends at the end of the scrutinee... no wait, the scrutinee's temporary is live across the whole match (in the arms that bind it). In the wildcard arm with no bindings, the temporary is dead after the scrutinee → the borrow ends. I think NLL handles this correctly and it compiles. Example:
```rust
match map.get_mut(k) {
    Some(x) => use x,
    None => map.insert(k, v),  // does this compile?
}
```
Hmm — `None => map.insert(...)` — the scrutinee's borrow is a temporary `Option<&mut V>`; in the None arm it isn't bound and isn't used → NLL lets the borrow end before the arm body. I think this pattern compiles fine under NLL (a common pattern: `match v.get_mut(..) { Some(x) => ..., None => v.insert(..) }`). Yes, this is a well-known pattern that works.

`key.leaf_decor.set_prefix(prefix)` — the leading comment/decoration is attached to the **key** (the `leaf_decor` of Key), not to the value! So even if we replace the value, the comment is preserved (the key is untouched). The problem is that `Table::insert` replaces the entire KeyValue including the key.

So the correct approach is: keep the existing KeyValue, replace only the value, and give the new value the old value's decor (the prefix is usually an empty whitespace, and the suffix is a trailing comment).

How to replace only the value of an existing key? Table doesn't expose a mutator for KeyValue... `table.get_mut(key)` → `&mut Item` — Item is `Item::Value(Value)`. We can replace the Value inside Item: `*v = new_value` (where v: &mut Value). This keeps the key (and its leaf_decor) as-is, and replaces the value. The old value's decor: prefix is usually "" (since the key holds the leading comment), and suffix might be a trailing comment like ` # note`. To be thorough, copy the old value's decor to the new value.

```rust
fn set_str(table: &mut toml_edit::Table, key: &str, value: &str) {
    let new_item = toml_edit::value(value.to_string());
    match table.get_mut(key) {
        Some(Item::Value(v)) if v.is_str() => {
            let decor = v.decor().clone();
            *v = ... new value with decor ...
        }
        _ => {
            table.insert(key, new_item);
        }
    }
}
```

    pub fn new(prefix: impl Into<RawString>, suffix: impl Into<RawString>) -> Self {

```diff
//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() && !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    set_str(pkg, "name", &m.package.name);
    set_str(pkg, "version", &m.package.version);
    set_str(pkg, "type", &m.package.kind);
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    set_str(build, "cpp_std", &m.build.cpp_std);
    set_str(build, "c_std", &m.build.c_std);
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        set_str(deps, name, version);
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Set a string key in a table. When the key already holds a string, only the
/// value is replaced — the key (which owns the line's leading comments in
/// toml_edit's model) and the value's trailing decoration are preserved.
/// Other cases insert a fresh key.
fn set_str(table: &mut toml_edit::Table, key: &str, value: &str) {
    match table.get_mut(key) {
        Some(Item::Value(v)) if v.is_str() => {
            let old_decor = v.decor().clone();
            let mut new_value =
                toml_edit::Value::String(toml_edit::Formatted::new(value.to_string()));
            let mut decor = new_value.decor_mut();
            if let Some(prefix) = old_decor.prefix() {
                decor.set_prefix(prefix.clone());
            }
            if let Some(suffix) = old_decor.suffix() {
                decor.set_suffix(suffix.clone());
            }
            *v = new_value;
        }
        _ => {
            table.insert(key, toml_edit::value(value.to_string()));
        }
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some('>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const FULL: &str = "\
[package]
name = \"demo\"
version = \"0.1.0\"
type = \"both\"

[build]
cpp_std = \"c++23\"
c_std = \"c11\"

[dependencies]
fmt = \"10.2.1\"
zeta = \">=1.0\"
";

    /// Deterministic scratch dir under target/ (gitignored) for file-based tests.
    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")
            .join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_full_manifest() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "bin");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_load_roundtrip() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-roundtrip");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_preserves_existing_comments() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-comments");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "# my project\n\
             [package]\n\
             name = \"demo\"\n\
             # pinned version\n\
             version = \"0.1.0\"\n\
             type = \"bin\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_validation_rules() {
        for ok in ["demo", "a", "foo-bar", "_x", "9lives", "a_b-c"] {
            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_name(bad), "{bad:?} should be invalid");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_rejects_bad_name_type_version() {
        for text in [
            "[package]\nname = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"Bad-Name\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\ntype = \"exe\"\n",
        ] {
            let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(text).unwrap();
            let err = m.validate().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(err.contains("help:"), "missing help hint in: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn conan_ref_branches() {
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", "10.2.1"), "fmt/10.2.1");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ">=10"), "fmt/[>=10]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "<=2"), "z/[<=2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "=1.2.3"), "z/[=1.2.3]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "^1"), "z/[^1]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }
}

## Assistant

`let mut decor = new_value.decor_mut();` — the `decor` variable holds a `&mut Decor`. To call `decor.set_prefix(...)` on `decor: &mut Decor`, the binding doesn't need to be `mut` (auto-deref: `(*decor).set_prefix(...)` — mutating through a `&mut` reference doesn't require the binding holding that reference to be `mut`. What's required is that the *referenced data* be mutable, and that's satisfied by the `&mut`). Exactly — `let x: &mut T = ...; x.method_needing_mut_self()` works even without `mut x`. That's because the binding isn't being reassigned.

Fix: remove the `mut` from `decor`.

```diff
//! CARRY.toml manifest: serde schema, load/save, validation, conan ref mapping.
//!
//! The manifest is the single source of truth for a carry project (DESIGN.md §4).
//! `save` goes through `toml_edit` so user comments and formatting survive edits
//! (the format-preserving `add`/`remove` commands build on this in T006).

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use toml_edit::Item;

const VERSION_DEFAULT: &str = "0.1.0";
const TYPE_DEFAULT: &str = "bin";
const CPP_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c++23";
const C_STD_DEFAULT: &str = "c11";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Manifest {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub package: Package,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub build: Build,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub dependencies: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Package {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub name: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_version")]
    pub version: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", default = "default_type")]
    pub kind: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Build {
    #[serde(default = "default_cpp_std")]
    pub cpp_std: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_c_std")]
    pub c_std: String,
}

fn default_version() -> String {
    VERSION_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_type() -> String {
    TYPE_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_cpp_std() -> String {
    CPP_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

fn default_c_std() -> String {
    C_STD_DEFAULT.to_string()
}

impl Default for Package {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            name: String::new(),
            version: default_version(),
            kind: default_type(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for Build {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            cpp_std: default_cpp_std(),
            c_std: default_c_std(),
        }
    }
}

impl Manifest {
    /// Read and parse `CARRY.toml` from `path`, then validate it.
    #[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the build pipeline (T004)
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self> {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
        let manifest: Self = toml::from_str(&text)
            .with_context(|| format!("parsing {}", path.display()))?;
        manifest.validate()?;
        Ok(manifest)
    }

    /// Write the manifest to `path` via `toml_edit`: an existing file is parsed
    /// first and updated in place (comments/formatting survive); a missing or
    /// empty file is written from scratch.
    pub fn save(&self, path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        let existing = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok();
        let mut doc: toml_edit::DocumentMut = match existing.as_deref() {
            Some(text) if !text.trim().is_empty() => text
                .parse()
                .with_context(|| format!("parsing existing {}", path.display()))?,
            _ => toml::to_string_pretty(self)
                .context("serializing manifest")?
                .parse()
                .context("serializing manifest")?,
        };
        apply_to_doc(self, &mut doc);
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() && !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", parent.display()))?;
        }
        std::fs::write(path, doc.to_string())
            .with_context(|| format!("writing {}", path.display()))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Check schema rules; returns errors with `help:` hints (DESIGN.md §8).
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        validate_name(&self.package.name)?;
        if self.package.version.is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "package.version is empty\n\
                 help: set a semver string, e.g. version = \"0.1.0\""
            );
        }
        match self.package.kind.as_str() {
            "bin" | "lib" | "both" => {}
            other => anyhow::bail!(
                "invalid package type {other:?}\n\
                 help: type must be one of: \"bin\", \"lib\", \"both\""
            ),
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Set every manifest value into an (existing or fresh) toml_edit document.
fn apply_to_doc(m: &Manifest, doc: &mut toml_edit::DocumentMut) {
    let pkg = ensure_table(doc, "package");
    set_str(pkg, "name", &m.package.name);
    set_str(pkg, "version", &m.package.version);
    set_str(pkg, "type", &m.package.kind);
    let build = ensure_table(doc, "build");
    set_str(build, "cpp_std", &m.build.cpp_std);
    set_str(build, "c_std", &m.build.c_std);
    let deps = ensure_table(doc, "dependencies");
    for (name, version) in &m.dependencies {
        set_str(deps, name, version);
    }
    let stale: Vec<String> = deps
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, _)| name.to_string())
        .filter(|name| !m.dependencies.contains_key(name))
        .collect();
    for name in stale {
        deps.remove(&name);
    }
}

/// Set a string key in a table. When the key already holds a string, only the
/// value is replaced — the key (which owns the line's leading comments in
/// toml_edit's model) and the value's trailing decoration are preserved.
/// Other cases insert a fresh key.
fn set_str(table: &mut toml_edit::Table, key: &str, value: &str) {
    match table.get_mut(key) {
        Some(Item::Value(v)) if v.is_str() => {
            let old_decor = v.decor().clone();
            let mut new_value =
                toml_edit::Value::String(toml_edit::Formatted::new(value.to_string()));
            let decor = new_value.decor_mut();
            if let Some(prefix) = old_decor.prefix() {
                decor.set_prefix(prefix.clone());
            }
            if let Some(suffix) = old_decor.suffix() {
                decor.set_suffix(suffix.clone());
            }
            *v = new_value;
        }
        _ => {
            table.insert(key, toml_edit::value(value.to_string()));
        }
    }
}

/// Return the named root table of `doc`, creating it if missing or non-table.
fn ensure_table<'a>(doc: &'a mut toml_edit::DocumentMut, key: &str) -> &'a mut toml_edit::Table {
    let is_table = doc.get(key).and_then(Item::as_table).is_some();
    if !is_table {
        doc.insert(key, Item::Table(toml_edit::Table::new()));
    }
    doc.get_mut(key)
        .and_then(Item::as_table_mut)
        .expect("table (re)created above")
}

/// A valid project name: `[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-]*` (DESIGN.md §4).
pub fn is_valid_name(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    let first = matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_');
    first && chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_' || c == '-')
}

/// Validate a project name, with a `help:` hint on failure.
pub fn validate_name(name: &str) -> Result<()> {
    anyhow::ensure!(
        is_valid_name(name),
        "invalid project name {name:?}\n\
         help: names must match [a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_-] \
         (lowercase letters, digits, '_', '-'; must not start with '-')"
    );
    Ok(())
}

/// Map a manifest dependency value to a Conan reference (DESIGN.md §4):
/// plain version → `pkg/ver`, range (`>` `<` `=` `^` `~` prefix) → `pkg/[range]`,
/// empty → `pkg/*`.
#[allow(dead_code)] // first used by the generator (T003)
pub fn conan_ref(pkg: &str, version: &str) -> String {
    match version.chars().next() {
        Some('>' | '<' | '=' | '^' | '~') => format!("{pkg}/[{version}]"),
        None => format!("{pkg}/*"),
        _ => format!("{pkg}/{version}"),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    const FULL: &str = "\
[package]
name = \"demo\"
version = \"0.1.0\"
type = \"both\"

[build]
cpp_std = \"c++23\"
c_std = \"c11\"

[dependencies]
fmt = \"10.2.1\"
zeta = \">=1.0\"
";

    /// Deterministic scratch dir under target/ (gitignored) for file-based tests.
    fn test_dir(name: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
        let dir = Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("target")
            .join("tmp")
            .join(name);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_full_manifest() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.name, "demo");
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "both");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.get("zeta").map(String::as_str), Some(">=1.0"));
        assert_eq!(m.dependencies.len(), 2);
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_minimal_manifest_with_defaults() {
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str("[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m.package.version, "0.1.0");
        assert_eq!(m.package.kind, "bin");
        assert_eq!(m.build.cpp_std, "c++23");
        assert_eq!(m.build.c_std, "c11");
        assert!(m.dependencies.is_empty());
        m.validate().unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_load_roundtrip() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-roundtrip");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(FULL).unwrap();
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m, m2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_preserves_existing_comments() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-comments");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "# my project\n\
             [package]\n\
             name = \"demo\"\n\
             # pinned version\n\
             version = \"0.1.0\"\n\
             type = \"bin\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(text.contains("# my project"), "header comment lost:\n{text}");
        assert!(text.contains("# pinned version"), "inline comment lost:\n{text}");
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn save_removes_stale_dependencies() {
        let dir = test_dir("manifest-stale-deps");
        let path = dir.join("CARRY.toml");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\n\n[dependencies]\nold = \"1.0\"\n",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let mut m = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        m.dependencies.clear();
        m.dependencies.insert("fmt".to_string(), "10.2.1".to_string());
        m.save(&path).unwrap();
        let m2 = Manifest::load(&path).unwrap();
        assert!(!m2.dependencies.contains_key("old"));
        assert_eq!(m2.dependencies.get("fmt").map(String::as_str), Some("10.2.1"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn name_validation_rules() {
        for ok in ["demo", "a", "foo-bar", "_x", "9lives", "a_b-c"] {
            assert!(is_valid_name(ok), "{ok:?} should be valid");
        }
        for bad in ["", "-leading", "Bad", "bad name", "bad/name", "böhm", "a?"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_name(bad), "{bad:?} should be invalid");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn validate_rejects_bad_name_type_version() {
        for text in [
            "[package]\nname = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"Bad-Name\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\nversion = \"\"\n",
            "[package]\nname = \"demo\"\ntype = \"exe\"\n",
        ] {
            let m: Manifest = toml::from_str(text).unwrap();
            let err = m.validate().unwrap_err().to_string();
            assert!(err.contains("help:"), "missing help hint in: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn conan_ref_branches() {
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", "10.2.1"), "fmt/10.2.1");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ">=10"), "fmt/[>=10]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "<=2"), "z/[<=2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "=1.2.3"), "z/[=1.2.3]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "^1"), "z/[^1]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("z", "~1.2"), "z/[~1.2]");
        assert_eq!(conan_ref("fmt", ""), "fmt/*");
    }
}
